# Encyclopedia index

611 entries, one file each in `entries/<slug>.md` (raw: `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dylanpetty378-arch/game-platform/refurbish/docs/encyclopedia/entries/<slug>.md`). Search: `python3 scripts/enc.py find <words>`. Aliases: stoneware-crocks→stoneware-jugs-crocks, 35mm-film-slrs→film-slr-cameras, cymbals→zildjian-cymbals, drum-cymbals→zildjian-cymbals, guitar-pedals→guitar-effects-pedals, boutique-guitar-pedals→guitar-effects-pedals, clocks-seth-thomas-mantel→mantel-clocks, lionel-transformers→lionel-postwar-transformers, lionel-transformers-and-postwar-trains→lionel-postwar-transformers, skateboard-decks-collectible→vintage-skateboards, vintage-skateboard-decks→vintage-skateboards, wooden-hand-planes→wooden-hand-planes, spinning-reels-vintage→spinning-and-conventional-reels.
